But within a few minutes, my mind is just off on three other job problems. I end up missing things I know I should have caught. So... yeah. I'm on my feet a lot anyway, moving around the site. So... it's hard to say.\n\nDoctor: Do you often interrupt others or blurt out answers at work?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I guess so. Sometimes. I just... I cut people off, I suppose. \n\nUsually, I think I already know where they're going with a question, so I just jump in. Answer it. But... then sometimes I realize I missed the actual point they were trying to make. Because I answered too fast. So... yeah. That happens.\n\nDoctor: Do you have trouble organizing your tasks or managing your time effectively at work?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I do. It's... it's a problem. \n\nMy truck is a wreck. Desk is the same. Even the notes on my phone are just... a mess. It's hard when everything hits at once, you know? RFIs, inspections, budget stuff, guys on the crew having issues. I just... I don't know what to tackle first. So I kind of bounce between them. And then... well, everything just ends up running late.\n\nDoctor: Do you have trouble staying focused or getting organized at home, or is it mainly at work?\n\nPatient: It's... both, I guess. \n\nAt work, it's gotten bad. I've missed some follow-ups, and... the site coordination hasn't been great. My boss actually pulled me aside about it. Said it was a formal concern. \n\nAnd at home... yeah. My wife is just... she's tired of it. I tell her I'll handle something, and then I don't. She has to keep reminding me. So... it's everywhere, really.\n\nDoctor: How is your relationship with your wife, has it been affected by your difficulties with focus and organization?\n\nPatient: Yeah, like I... like I just said. It's not great. She's just really worn down from it. Having to constantly remind me about things I told her I'd handle... it takes a toll on us. And with everything going on at work—like I mentioned, getting that formal warning over the missed follow-ups and the site coordination... it's just bad everywhere right now. Two years and a couple of months. \n\nThings shifted at work back then. The schedule changed, got a lot more chaotic. That's when it really started messing with my job. Before that, I don't know, I got by. But since then... yeah. About two years.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel restless or fidgety at work?\n\nPatient: I mean... I don't really know. Not really, I guess. I'm on my feet a lot anyway, moving around the site. So... it's hard to say.\n\nDoctor: Do you often interrupt others or blurt out answers at work?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I guess so. Sometimes. I just... I cut people off, I suppose. \n\nUsually, I think I already know where they're going with a question, so I just jump in. Answer it. But... then sometimes I realize I missed the actual point they were trying to make. Because I answered too fast. So... yeah. That happens.\n\nDoctor: Do you have trouble organizing your tasks or managing your time effectively at work?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I do. It's... it's a problem. \n\nMy truck is a wreck. Desk is the same. Even the notes on my phone are just... a mess. It's hard when everything hits at once, you know? RFIs, inspections, budget stuff, guys on the crew having issues. I just... I don't know what to tackle first. So I kind of bounce between them. It's not really a situation where they'd notice if I'm organized or whatever.
